<figure class="vibe-hero"><a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/vibes/bikeways.webp"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/figs/vibes/bikeways.webp" alt="AASHTO Bike Guide (5th Ed.) — Real-World Context" /></a><figcaption><strong>AASHTO Bike Guide (5th Ed.) — Real-World Context</strong> <em>National bikeway facility selection, Class I–IV cross-sections &amp; intersection design · Illustrative street-level context (not a construction detail)</em></figcaption></figure>

<div class="srcbar">5th Edition, December 2024 (incorporates December 2024 Errata) &middot; 626 pages &middot; ISBN 978-1-56051-820-4 &middot; 16 chapters, 774 sections, 256 figures, 50 tables</div>

AASHTO's national bicycle facility design guide (5th Edition, December 2024; supersedes the 2012 4th Edition). Covers bikeway selection, elements of design, shared use paths, separated bike lanes, side paths, bicycle boulevards, conventional/buffered bike lanes, signals, interchanges, roundabouts, structures, and wayfinding across 16 chapters. National guidance only: in California, Caltrans HDM Chapter 1000, DIB 89-02, and CA MUTCD 2026 Part 9 govern.

<div class="note-box"><b>California precedence (SHC &sect; 891):</b> AASHTO GBF-5 is national engineering guidance. In California, <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/bulletins/caltrans-hdm-ch1000/">Caltrans HDM Chapter 1000</a> (rev. 12/16/2024), <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/bulletins/caltrans-dib-89-02/">Caltrans DIB 89-02</a> (Class IV), and <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/ca-mutcd/9a/">CA MUTCD 2026 Part 9</a> govern. See the <a href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/bulletins/bikeway-design-comparison/">Bikeway Design Comparison</a> for side-by-side differences and California overrides. Source: <a href="https://store.transportation.org/item/publicationdetail/5371">AASHTO GBF-5</a> &middot; <a href="https://downloads.transportation.org/GBF-5-Errata.pdf">December 2024 Errata (PDF)</a>.</div>

<script>
(() => {
  const tabs = document.getElementById('gbf-tabs');
  const inp = document.getElementById('gbf-q');
  const rows = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#gbf-tbl tbody tr'));
  let kind = 'all';
  const apply = () => {
    const q = (inp.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    for (const r of rows) {
      const okK = kind === 'all' || r.dataset.kind === kind;
      const okQ = !q || r.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      r.style.display = (okK && okQ) ? '' : 'none';
    }
  };
  tabs.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest('button[data-k]');
    if (!b) return;
    kind = b.dataset.k;
    tabs.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('on', x === b));
    apply();
  });
  inp.addEventListener('input', apply);
})();
</script>
